Ah, I see exactly what's wrong! The massive SQL migration script creates the `user_role` ENUM twice with different values. 

First it creates: `'super_admin', 'company_admin', 'kitchen_staff', 'driver', 'shopping_staff'`

But later in the script, it expects: `'admin', 'kitchen', 'driver', 'client', 'cleaning', 'shopping', 'owner', 'super_admin', 'shopping_staff', 'cleaning_staff', 'kitchen_staff'`

I will fix this conflict by making sure the `user_role` ENUM is defined with all possible roles that the application's 125+ tables need.
